Conclusion: 循环学习范式结合混合专家可有效用于跨域统一超高清视频复原，以极低参数量实现多任务高性能。

Abstract: Recently, unified high-definition image restoration has attracted considerable attention; however, existing models tend to excessively increase their depth in pursuit of improved generalization, which often yields only limited gains. Meanwhile, loop-based learning paradigms have drawn widespread attention due to their low parameter counts and strong regression capability, as exemplified by GPT-6 and looped Transformers. In this paper, we introduce the loop learning paradigm to address restoration tasks that require cross-domain learning. Specifically, we propose LoopMoEVR, a loop-based mixture-of-experts model capable of handling degraded ultra-high-definition (UHD) inputs. First, a degradation-conditioned low-rank loop embedding is designed to construct input-dependent stage conditions. Second, a spatio-temporal iterative adaptive normalization module, termed IterAda3DN, is developed to fuse local features with global loop context, thereby performing position-wise affine modulation. Finally, the expert branches further integrate the attention-updated local and global video states with the loop conditions to generate dedicated modulation parameters, while an input-conditioned depth predictor adaptively configures the number of loop iterations. With only approximately 0.884M trainable parameters, the proposed model uniformly handles UHD video dehazing, deraining, denoising, and low-light enhancement tasks, achieving state-of-the-art restoration performance on both public benchmarks and real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [103] [PCLM: Small-target localization with frozen CLIP via prototype contrast and local magnification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05115)
*Zhipeng Ye,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PCLM方法，利用冻结CLIP编码器，通过原型对比与局部放大实现小目标定位，在多个数据集上超越文本条件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小目标在视觉-语言编码器中占据的patch很少，导致空间特征常将目标外观与周围内容混合，难以精确定位。

Method: PCLM为支持条件定位方法：每类用5张掩码支持图像定义前景与背景原型（等权区域特征），其差值提供查询patch的共享评分方向；将9个重叠查询窗口放大并独立编码以更密集采样小目标，再通过重投影与覆盖率平均融合成连续定位图。

Result: 在VOC、COCO、ADE20K和Oxford-IIIT Pets的5,047个小目标查询上，平均像素AP均高于所有评估的文本条件定位基线；相对最强场景数据集基线提升5.63至13.23个百分点；局部放大比全画布放大的场景小目标AP提升4.51至5.68点。

Conclusion: 原型对比增强局部观测收益；类别方向仅占2 KiB且不随支持数量增长，可跨数据集迁移；更多样例可细化类别估计而不增加表示大小或查询时评分成本。

Abstract: Small targets occupy few patches in a vision-language encoder, so spatial features often mix object appearance with surrounding content. We propose Prototype Contrast and Local Magnification (PCLM), a support-conditioned localization method that uses a frozen CLIP encoder. Five masked support images per class define foreground and background prototypes through equally weighted regional features. Their difference provides a shared scoring direction for query patches, explicitly comparing target evidence with the demonstrated background. Nine overlapping query windows are enlarged and encoded independently to sample small targets more densely. Reprojection and coverage averaging combine their scores into a continuous localization map. The class direction occupies 2 KiB regardless of support count and transfers unchanged across datasets with mapped categories. On 5,047 small-target queries from VOC, COCO, ADE20K and Oxford-IIIT Pets, PCLM achieves higher mean pixel AP than every evaluated text-conditioned localization baseline on each dataset under our evaluation protocol. Gains over the strongest scene-dataset baselines range from 5.63 to 13.23 percentage points. At comparable measured latency, local magnification improves scene small-target AP by 4.51 to 5.68 points over whole-canvas enlargement. Factorial experiments show that prototype contrast increases the benefit of local observation, including under matched image-coordinate filtering. Support-budget experiments show that additional examples refine category estimation without increasing representation size or query-time scoring cost.

</details>


### [104] [Representation--Behavior Alignment for Explainable Weakly-Supervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05129)
*Chao Huang,Pengfei Wei,Kaige Li,Chengliang Liu,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出表征–行为对齐（RBA）方法，通过极少量参数适配中层注意力，缓解多模态大模型在视频异常检测中"隐状态含判别信息但决策未充分利用"的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型虽能让视频异常检测更可解释，但其最终决策并不总能充分利用隐状态中的判别信息，即表征–行为错位问题。

Method: 将错位分解为容量成分（判别信息未被聚合到读出位置）与方向成分（最优与原生正常–异常轴的角度失配）；残差流追踪发现两者由注意力而非MLP更新主导，故提出参数高效的RBA，仅用视频级标签适配相关中后层，更新约0.012%骨干参数。

Result: 在多个视频异常检测基准与多种MLLM骨干上，方向成分占主导；原生轴可分性在若干中后注意力层显著上升；三个基准实验中RBA提升了原生读出性能并改善了决策方向与判别表征的对齐。

Conclusion: RBA以极低参数代价实现表征与行为对齐，并通过单一生成过程同时产出异常决策与解释。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) provide a natural way to make video anomaly detection more explainable. However, their final decisions do not always fully use the discriminative information contained in their hidden states, an issue we refer to as representation--behavior misalignment. We decompose this gap into a capacity component that measures discriminative information never aggregated into the readout position, and a directional component that measures the angular mismatch between the optimal and the native normal--abnormal axis at that position. Across multiple video anomaly detection benchmarks and MLLM backbones the directional component dominates, and residual-stream tracing shows that native-axis separability rises sharply in several mid-to-late attention layers. Because both components are governed by attention rather than MLP updates, we propose Representation--Behavior Alignment (RBA), a parameter-efficient method that adapts those layers using video-level labels alone while updating about 0.012\% of the backbone parameters. Experiments on three benchmarks show that RBA improves native-readout performance and better aligns the model's decision direction with discriminative representations, and it produces anomaly decisions and explanations through a single generative process.

</details>


### [105] [RoMod: Temporal Routing Modulation via Mixture-of-Experts for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05131)
*Chao Huang,Pengfei Wei,Benfeng Wang,Chengliang Liu,Wei Wang,Li Shen,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型为切入点，揭示多模态大模型中间层特征在视频异常检测（VAD）中的判别力来源，并提出高效的RoMod框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型中间层特征在视频异常检测上表现优异，但其判别力的来源尚不清晰，作者希望借助MoE显式的专家结构与稀疏激活特性来解读内部计算机制。

Method: 在完全冻结骨干、无需额外训练的条件下分析MoE内部专家行为；发现异常相关证据集中于少数专家，这些专家跨层反复出现、自发分工于不同异常类型，构成动态路由子网络，且其影响最强的输出通道恰是含异常信息最多的隐维度。据此提出RoMod框架，仅用5%弱标注视频训练，包含路由调制融合模块RoMF（用路由信号自适应重校准语义隐通道，并防止路由分支绕过语义特征单独预测）与路由感知时序网络RoTN（刻画异常从发生到持续到终止的时序演化）。

Result: 在三个基准上，RoMod达到最先进性能，同时运行速度显著快于同等规模的稠密骨干网络。

Conclusion: 路由统计可作为补充语义特征的内部异常线索；基于此的RoMod以极低标注成本实现高效且领先的VAD性能。

Abstract: Intermediate-layer features from multimodal large language models have shown strong potential for video anomaly detection (VAD), yet the origin of their discriminative power remains unclear. We study this question using sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, whose explicit expert structure and sparse activation make their internal computation easier to inspect. With a fully frozen backbone and no additional training, we find that anomaly-related evidence is concentrated in a small set of experts. These experts recur across layers, spontaneously specialize in different anomaly types, and together form a dynamic routing subnetwork. We further show that the output channels most strongly influenced by these experts are also the hidden dimensions that contain the most anomaly-relevant information. Routing statistics can therefore serve as an internal anomaly cue that complements semantic features.Based on these findings, we propose RoMod, an efficient VAD framework trained with only \(5\%\) of weakly labeled videos. RoMod includes a Routing-Modulated Fusion module, RoMF, and a Routing-aware Temporal Network, RoTN. RoMF uses routing signals to adaptively recalibrate hidden semantic channels. Its design also prevents the routing branch from bypassing semantic features and making predictions on its own. RoTN captures the temporal evolution of anomalies from onset to persistence and termination. Experiments on three benchmarks show that RoMod achieves state-of-the-art performance while running substantially faster than dense backbones of comparable size.

</details>


### [106] [How Does Geometry Enter Generated Motion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05135)
*Weihan Li,Junhao Wu,Yuhan Song,Xiaofeng Lin,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频生成模型能感知几何形状并生成相应运动，但其状态演化系统性地偏离固定物理定律，行为更像"几何条件运动合成"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定物理定律下，场景可见几何决定运动如何变化。作者想探究视频生成器是否真正实现了这一几何-运动关系，还是仅做出表面相似的运动。

Method: 固定物理定律与初始状态，仅改变首帧所绘几何（在匹配的轨道与挡板族内对比），并将每条生成轨迹与模拟器对该几何的预测进行比较。采用配对干预，每次只改动一个变量：局部隆起、障碍高度、提示词文字、片段长度。

Result: 在九个图像转视频模型中，几何被保留并塑造运动（球速跟随轨道起伏）。但物理状态未能延续：平均坡度几乎不使球加速、连续接触无法通过一致状态复合、球前方的编辑在球到达前就改变其运动、球会爬过高于释放点的障碍。文本强烈控制目的地，片段长度强烈控制时序（在测试的开源权重模型中）。该模式在实拍首帧下依然存在。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成表现为"几何条件运动合成"，其状态演化系统性偏离固定物理定律，即能响应几何却无法维持一致的物理状态。

Abstract: Under a fixed physical law, the visible geometry of a scene determines how motion must change. We ask how video generators realize this relationship. We fix the law and the initial state and change only the geometry drawn in the first frame, within matched families of tracks and deflectors, and compare each generated trajectory with the simulator prediction for that geometry. Paired interventions change one thing at a time: a local bump, the height of a barrier, the words of the prompt, the length of the clip. Across nine image-to-video models, geometry is preserved and shapes the motion: the speed of the ball follows the drawn undulation of a track. A physical state would carry this response forward, and here the generated motion parts from the law. The mean slope barely accelerates the ball, successive contacts fail to compose through a consistent state, an edit ahead of the ball alters its motion before it arrives, and the ball climbs over barriers higher than its release point. Two global conditions organize the global trajectory: text strongly controls the destination, while clip length strongly controls timing in the open-weight models tested. The pattern persists with photographed first frames. Current video generation thus behaves as geometry-conditioned motion synthesis whose evolution of state differs systematically from that of a fixed physical law.

</details>


### [107] [SemCam: Semantic Camera Motion Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05141)
*Janna Bruner,Omer Talmi,Ianir Ideses,Lior Fritz,Lior Wolf,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出语义相机运动控制任务及方法SemCam，通过参考视频与运动标签实现主体相对相机行为控制，成功率68.6%，优于最强基线Vista4D的45.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有视频中控制相机相对运动主体极具挑战：维持正面视角等行为需相机随主体位置与朝向动态调整，目标轨迹难以预先设定；而现有相机可控的视频到视频方法依赖显式轨迹或参考运动，无法直接表达这种动态的相机—主体关系。

Method: 提出语义相机运动控制这一新型视频到视频任务：以参考视频和目标运动标签指定期望的主体相对相机行为，无需显式目标轨迹。SemCam将共享基低秩适配与运动条件调制相结合，并引入背景一致性损失以提升参考与目标视频共同可见区域的保真度。

Result: 构建了涵盖八种语义相机行为的661对配对视频，并在独立的109场景基准上使用主体相对运动指标、外观指标及用户研究进行评估。SemCam取得68.6%的语义运动成功率，高于最强基线Vista4D的45.3%，同时保持相当的主体身份保持能力。

Conclusion: SemCam能够在无需显式轨迹的情况下，依据运动标签实现主体相对的语义相机运动控制，并保持源内容与主体身份，验证了语义相机运动控制任务的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Controlling the camera relative to a moving subject in an existing video is challenging: behaviors such as maintaining a frontal view require the camera to adapt to the subject's changing position and orientation, making the desired trajectory difficult to specify in advance. Existing camera-controlled video-to-video methods typically rely on explicit trajectories or reference motions, which do not directly express these dynamic camera--subject relationships. We introduce semantic camera motion control, a novel video-to-video task in which a reference video and a target motion label specify the desired subject-relative camera behavior without an explicit target trajectory. Our method, SemCam, learns to realize this behavior while preserving source content. It combines shared-basis low-rank adaptation with motion-conditioned modulation, while a background-consistency loss encourages fidelity in regions visible in both reference and target videos. We construct 661 paired videos covering eight semantic camera behaviors and evaluate on a separate 109-scene benchmark using subject-relative motion metrics, appearance measures, and a user study. SemCam achieves a semantic-motion success rate of 68.6%, compared with 45.3% for Vista4D, the strongest evaluated baseline, while maintaining comparable subject identity preservation.

</details>


### [108] [Construction and Evaluation of Machine Learning Models for Near-Real-Time Fire Detection from MTG FCI Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05154)
*Asaf Vanunu,Boaz Nadler,Arnon Karnieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了机器学习模型在MTG FCI静止卫星1km与2km配置下的近实时火点检测性能，并与阈值算法对比，发现1km模型显著优于2km版本及业务化阈值产品，且更早探测到火灾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静止卫星观测对野火检测与监测至关重要，需评估机器学习模型在MTG FCI近实时火点检测中的表现，并与传统阈值算法进行比较。

Method: 使用VIIRS火点参考数据，在涵盖欧洲、非洲和中东的多样生态区训练与评估1km和2km空间配置的机器学习模型，并与阈值型算法对比。

Result: 1km模型显著优于2km版本和业务化阈值产品，F1分数最高提升0.36；1km模型对小火灾的检测概率更高；可较基线产品提前最多260分钟探测到火灾。

Conclusion: 1km机器学习模型在近实时火点检测中性能优越，具备更早预警能力，且训练模型已公开以支持开源应用。

Abstract: Geostationary satellite observations are important for wildfire detection and monitoring. The current study evaluates machine learning models for MTG FCI near-real-time fire detection in 1- and 2-km spatial configurations and compares them with threshold-based algorithms. The models were trained and evaluated using VIIRS fire reference data across diverse ecological regions in Europe, Africa, and the Middle East. The key results are that 1-km models significantly outperform both their 2-km variants and operational threshold products. The constructed 1-km models achieved F1 scores higher by up to 0.36 compared to baseline products. Importantly, the 1-km models detected small fires with higher probability compared to competing models. Finally, the models robustly detected fires up to 260 min earlier than baseline products. To support opensource applications, our trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [109] [Recurrent Latent Visual Search for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05185)
*Kaiyu Wu,Beichen Zheng,Weiyao Huang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReLaViS，一种在单次交互中循环进行潜在视觉搜索的 GUI grounding 方法，通过空间搜索分布聚合视觉证据并循环反馈，提升密集布局下的定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding 是视觉语言模型驱动 GUI 智能体的关键能力，但单步定位在小元素和密集布局场景下表现不佳。现有多步视觉搜索方法要么依赖与视觉空间不对齐的文本推理，要么需要与外部视觉工具进行昂贵的多轮交互。

Method: ReLaViS 将多步视觉搜索内化为模型内部的显式空间过程：每步中，空间搜索头利用隐藏状态查询截图的视觉 token，生成显式表示搜索焦点的空间搜索分布；该分布聚合视觉 token 为潜在视觉证据，并循环反馈作为下一步的输入嵌入以引导后续搜索。同时通过平坦元素标注构建的轨迹引入 GUI 感知的由粗到细归纳偏置，监督从全局界面到中间元素组再到目标的搜索过程。

Result: 基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 构建，ReLaViS 将 ScreenSpot-Pro 准确率提升 3.1 个百分点至 56.3%，推理 FLOPs 仅增加 3.5%，并在全部五个基准上优于匹配的单步基线。

Conclusion: ReLaViS 以低成本实现了单次交互内的显式多步视觉搜索，有效改善 GUI grounding 性能，验证了将空间搜索过程内化于模型的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: GUI grounding is a critical capability for GUI agents powered by vision-language models, helping them execute user instructions by locating the corresponding elements in screenshots. Single-step grounding struggles with small elements and dense layouts, motivating multi-step visual search. However, existing approaches commonly rely on textual reasoning misaligned with visual space or costly multi-round interactions with external visual tools. To make multi-step visual search an explicit spatial process within the model, we propose ReLaViS, which performs Recurrent Latent Visual Search in a single interaction round. At each step, a spatial search head uses the hidden state to query the screenshot's visual tokens, producing a spatial search distribution that explicitly represents the search focus. This distribution then aggregates the visual tokens into latent visual evidence, which is recurrently fed back as the next input embedding to condition subsequent search. We further introduce a GUI-aware coarse-to-fine inductive bias through trajectories constructed from flat element annotations, supervising search from the global interface through intermediate element groups to the target. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, ReLaViS improves ScreenSpot-Pro accuracy by 3.1 percentage points to 56.3% with only a 3.5% increase in inference FLOPs and outperforms the matched single-step baseline on all five benchmarks.

</details>


### [110] [Order Matters: Competition-Guided Query Ordering for RNN-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05191)
*Shengjian Wu,Li Sun,Yu Shangguan,Qingli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DETRNN，一个即插即用模块，将DETR中无序的目标查询按置信度与相似度排序，再用RNN进行递归细化，从而在解码器内部建模查询间的竞争关系，减少冗余预测、稳定训练并提升检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DETR类检测器使用一对一二分匹配进行训练，无需NMS即可实现端到端集合预测。但由于缺乏显式的去重机制，多个查询仍可能对同一目标产生高度相似的假设，导致训练不稳定、预测不够果断。作者受NMS顺序化处理思想的启发，希望将这种顺序竞争机制引入解码器内部。

Method: 提出DETRNN模块：首先根据先验预测构建显式的、基于置信度与相似度的排序，将无序的目标查询转变为具有竞争感知的序列；然后沿该顺序用RNN对查询进行递归细化，从而在解码器内部建模查询之间的竞争关系。

Result: 在多种DETR类检测器上的实验表明，该方法能持续带来性能提升，且效率与原有方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过有序的递归细化，DETRNN能减少冗余预测、稳定优化过程并提升最终检测精度，且作为即插即用模块具有较好的通用性与效率表现。

Abstract: DETR-style detectors use one-to-one bipartite matching during training to assign object queries to ground-truth objects, enabling end-to-end set prediction without non-maximum suppression (NMS). However, without an explicit de-duplication procedure, multiple queries can still produce highly similar hypotheses for the same object, making training unstable and predictions less decisive. Inspired by the sequential ordering of NMS, we propose DETRNN, a plug-and-play module that turns unordered object queries into a competition-aware sequence for recurrent refinement. DETRNN builds an explicit confidence-and-similarity based order from prior predictions, then refines queries with an RNN along this order to model competition inside the decoder. This ordered recurrent refinement reduces redundant predictions, stabilizes optimization, and improves final detection accuracy. Experiments on multiple DETR-style detectors show consistent gains with comparable efficiency.

</details>


### [111] [Long-MDR: Long-Context Reinforcement Learning for Multimodal Deep-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05195)
*On Tai Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Long-MDR 提出三组件训练配方，将在线多模态研究智能体的强化学习训练扩展到 128k 上下文与 75+ 工具交互轮次，在 50 轮评测预算下，Long-MDR-9B 在六个基准中的五个上位列同规模（7B-9B）智能体第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代多模态研究智能体需要在长期研究历史（多次网页搜索、视觉证据检查、假设回溯、数万 token 的多模态上下文）上进行推理，但当前多模态智能体的在线强化学习仍局限于较短的上下文与交互视野。直接扩展到长视野训练会暴露两个实际问题：训练早期弱策略浪费大量交互预算导致 rollout 昂贵而收益甚微；训练后期策略熵在性能饱和前就崩塌，过早终止有效学习。

Method: 提出 Long-MDR，一套面向长视野在线多模态 RL 的三组件训练配方：1）On-Policy Distillation Warmup（同策略蒸馏预热）；2）Progressive Horizon Expansion（渐进式视野扩展）；3）Entropy-Triggered Rescue（熵触发救援）。三个组件协同以提升长视野 RL 的学习效率与稳定性。

Result: 将在线 RL 训练推进到 128k 上下文和 75+ 工具交互轮次，据称是首个在 128k 上下文训练、且首个在 75 轮工具交互视野下训练的在线多模态深度研究 RL 研究。在 50 轮评测预算下，RL 训练的 Long-MDR-9B 在对比的 7B-9B 智能体中于六个基准中的五个上排名第一。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过针对性的训练配方设计，可以在直接训练缓慢且昂贵的超长视野场景中实现持续的性能提升，为长视野多模态研究智能体的在线 RL 训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The next generation of multimodal research agents must reason over long-lived research histories rather than short model completions. During a single task, an agent may repeatedly search the web, inspect visual evidence, revisit earlier hypotheses, and accumulate tens of thousands of tokens of multimodal context. Despite this trend, online RL for multimodal research agents remains largely confined to shorter contexts and interaction horizons. We push online RL training to 128k context and 75+ tool-interaction turns. To our knowledge, this is the first online multimodal deep-research RL study trained at 128k context, and the first trained with a 75 tool-turn horizon. Scaling to this regime exposes several practical limitations of conventional RL training. Early in training, weak policies make poor use of large interaction budgets, causing expensive rollouts with little reward improvement. Later, policy entropy can collapse before performance has saturated, prematurely ending useful learning. We introduce Long-MDR, a three-component training recipe designed specifically for this setting: On-Policy Distillation Warmup, Progressive Horizon Expansion, and Entropy-Triggered Rescue. Together, these techniques improve both the learning efficiency and stability of long-horizon RL, enabling continued gains in a regime where direct training is slow and costly. At a 50-turn evaluation budget, our RL-trained Long-MDR-9B ranks first on five of six benchmarks among the compared 7B-9B agents.

</details>


### [112] [F$^2$ SLAM: Turning Feed-Forward Geometry into Persistent Factors for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05207)
*Zhisong Xu,Fan Zhu,Jiawei Qian,Ziyu Chen,Zhenjun Zhao,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: F2SLAM将前馈3D模型的多视图几何先验直接转化为优化原生的目标权重测量，嵌入持久稠密因子图，通过高低频双流共同约束统一稠密光束法平差，显著提升轨迹估计与稠密重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线SLAM主要依赖局部测量，长序列中会累积漂移；而前馈3D模型虽提供强多视图几何先验，但现有方法仅将其作为外部几何状态事后对齐或融合，使更广的多视图证据游离于优化器之外，无法参与SLAM状态的精化。

Method: 提出F2SLAM，将前馈几何直接转换为附着于持久稠密因子图的优化原生目标权重测量。采用双流结构：高频流维持局部跟踪约束与图连通性；低频流利用更广的多视图上下文，在状态一致性检查后选择性刷新已有测量。两流通过单一稠密光束法平差共同约束位姿、逆深度及可选的相机内参。

Result: 在多个基准上均表现出一致且强的轨迹估计能力，并在标定与非标定设置下改善了稠密重建。尤其非标定配置在Replica数据集上将平均ATE RMSE从最强前馈基线的0.030 m降至0.002 m。

Conclusion: F2SLAM通过将前馈几何内化为优化原生约束，弥合了前馈多视图先验与在线SLAM优化器之间的鸿沟，实现了更优的轨迹估计与稠密重建，并在标定与非标定场景下均具有良好表现。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models provide strong multi-view geometric priors, while on- line simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) relies mainly on local mea- surements and can accumulate drift over long sequences. Existing attempts to combine the two typically treat feed-forward predictions as an external geomet- ric state that is aligned or fused with the online estimate after the fact, which keeps broader multi-view evidence outside the optimizer that refines the SLAM state. We present F2SLAM, which instead converts feed-forward geometry di- rectly into optimization-native target-weight measurements attached to a persis- tent dense factor graph. A high-frequency stream maintains local tracking con- straints and graph connectivity, while a low-frequency stream uses wider multi- view context to selectively refresh existing measurements after a state-consistency check. Both streams constrain the same poses, inverse depths, and optional cam- era intrinsics through a single dense bundle adjustment. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistently strong trajectory estimation and improved dense reconstruction in both calibrated and uncalibrated settings. Notably, the uncalibrated configuration reduces the average ATE RMSE from 0.030 m for the strongest feed-forward baseline to 0.002 m on the Replica dataset.

</details>


### [113] [PixReenact: Pixel-Conditioned Causal Video Diffusion for Streaming Head-Avatar Reenactment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05233)
*Gavriel Habib,Dvir Samuel,Or Shimshi,Rami Ben-Ari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Streaming head-avatar reenactment aims to animate a reference image according to a live driving video, requiring robust motion transfer, long-term identity stability, and low latency. Existing methods often rely on specialized identity or motion representations, which can discard useful visual information and inherit failure modes from external extractors. In addition, many recent diffusion-based reenactment methods use offline, clip-based generation, jointly processing and denoising an entire video clip before producing its output, making continuous low-latency streaming difficult. We introduce PixReenact, a pixel-conditioned streaming reenactment framework built on causal video diffusion. PixReenact conditions directly on VAE-encoded reference and driving frames, without specialized identity or motion representations. To separate reference identity from driver motion, we train with cross-identity pseudo supervision together with corrective objectives anchored to the original reference and driving inputs. Long self-rollouts reduce autoregressive drift, while state-aware dual-teacher distillation separately addresses cold-start and steady-state generation. Across three cross-identity benchmarks and a long-horizon streaming benchmark, PixReenact demonstrates robust cross-identity reenactment, particularly under challenging conditions such as extreme viewpoints, occlusions, and pronounced facial expressions, while maintaining the reference identity over long streams. A 4-NFE rolling student continuously emits four frames per update with a mean emission latency of 239 ms.

</details>


### [114] [ArticuTable: Generating Instance-Level Interactive Rigid-Articulated 3D Tabletop Scenes from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05249)
*Kai Lv,Yibo Yin,Lijun Guo,Heng Fan,Kaihao Zhang,Xingping Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArticuTable 是一个单图像桌面场景 3D 重建框架，可同时恢复可执行的部件级关节运动与输入视角一致的场景布局，并贡献了 100 个可仿真桌面场景数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图桌面重建方法虽能恢复看似合理的几何结构，但通常将物体表示为整体刚体，交互仅限整体刚性运动，无法进行部件级关节操作；同时，单视角观测往往对应多种姿态-尺度配置，难以保证重建布局与输入视图一致。

Method: 提出生成鲁棒关节建模（GRAM），结合多模态大语言模型引导的关节拟合与语义状态推理，从粗糙的整体代理网格中恢复可靠关节参数与有效运动范围，转化为可执行的关节化资产；提出渐进式语义-几何场景配准（PSGSR），在度量、平面和输入视角约束下逐步缩小姿态-尺度搜索空间，并通过结构感知的语义对应解决朝向歧义。

Result: 构建了 ArticuTable-100 数据集，包含 100 个可直接仿真的桌面场景；大量评估（含用户研究）在视觉保真度、输入视角一致性、关节质量、物理合理性和仿真可用性方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: ArticuTable 通过部件级关节恢复与视角一致布局配准，提升了单图桌面重建的可交互性与仿真可用性。

Abstract: Embodied agents benefit from 3D environments that combine visual fidelity to real-world observations with physical interactivity. Existing single-image tabletop reconstruction methods recover plausible scene geometry but typically represent objects as monolithic rigid bodies, limiting interaction to whole-object rigid motion and precluding executable part-level articulation. Meanwhile, recovering a scene layout consistent with the input view remains challenging because a single observation may admit multiple plausible pose-scale configurations. We present ArticuTable, a single-image 3D tabletop reconstruction framework that recovers both executable part-level articulation and an input-view-consistent scene layout. For object modeling, we introduce generation-robust articulation modeling (GRAM), which combines joint fitting guided by a multimodal large language model with semantic state reasoning to recover reliable joint parameters and valid motion ranges from imperfect monolithic proxy meshes, thereby converting them into executable articulated assets. For scene layout, we introduce progressive semantic-geometric scene registration (PSGSR), which progressively narrows the pose-scale search space under complementary metric, planar, and input-view constraints and resolves orientation ambiguity through structure-aware semantic correspondences, yielding a scene layout consistent with the input view. We further contribute ArticuTable-100, a curated collection of 100 simulation-ready tabletop scenes. Extensive evaluation, including a user study, demonstrates strong performance across visual fidelity, input-view consistency, articulation quality, physical plausibility, and simulation readiness.

</details>


### [115] [MGPO: Manifold-Guided Diffusion Alignment for Task-Aware Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05252)
*Yunyi Chen,Chenru Wang,Xinyi Ye,Zexin Zheng,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Manifold-Guided Policy Optimization (MGPO)，将扩散式数据集蒸馏重构为多目标强化学习问题，通过像素空间判别奖励与潜空间几何奖励（基于类别 MST）实现双空间对齐，显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式数据集蒸馏存在目标错配：似然驱动的扩散模型偏重密度近似而非下游任务所需的判别边界；同时仅依赖密度还导致几何覆盖丢失，生成样本坍缩到少数高密度模式，无法覆盖流形结构多样性。

Method: 将数据集蒸馏重构为多目标强化学习问题，实现双空间对齐：像素空间判别奖励强制类间可分性；潜空间几何奖励由类别级最小生成树（MST）引导，促使生成潜变量覆盖每类的稀疏几何骨架。奖励模块化设计可通过替换冻结的任务奖励模型扩展到目标检测、分割等结构化任务。

Result: 提供理想化理论分析支撑 MST 奖励，包括 Hausdorff 近似界与独立于数据集规模的子采样界。大量实验表明 MGPO 持续优于现有方法，低预算分割任务上 mIoU 提升 +8.0%。

Conclusion: 通过双空间对齐的多目标强化学习框架，MGPO 同时解决了判别性不足与几何覆盖丢失两大问题，验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based dataset distillation (DD) suffers from a fundamental objective mismatch: likelihood-driven diffusion models prioritize density approximation over the discriminative decision boundaries required for downstream tasks. Beyond semantic mismatch, relying solely on density also leads to geometric coverage loss, where generated samples collapse into a few high-density modes and fail to cover the manifold's structural diversity. We propose Manifold-Guided Policy Optimization (MGPO), which reformulates DD as a multi-objective reinforcement learning problem and achieves Dual-Space Alignment via a pixel-space discriminative reward and a latent-space geometric reward guided by a class-wise Minimum Spanning Tree (MST). The discriminative reward enforces class separability, while the MST-based geometric reward encourages generated latents to cover a sparse geometric skeleton of each class, jointly addressing both failure modes. We further provide an idealized analysis that motivates the MST-based reward, including a Hausdorff approximation bound and a subsampling bound independent of the dataset size. The reward-modular design extends to structured tasks such as object detection and segmentation by substituting the frozen task reward model. Extensive experiments show MGPO consistently outperforms existing methods, including a +8.0% mIoU gain on segmentation under low-budget settings.

</details>


### [116] [Hybrid-Basis Feature Forecasting for Diffusion Sampling Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05254)
*Kai-Liang Cheng,Yuan-Yuan Cheng,Yu-fan Jin,Xiao-Ming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HybridFF，一种免训练、即插即用的扩散采样加速框架，通过多基函数预测与融合权重策略实现质量-速度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样速度慢，需要加速。建模特征演化时需同时捕捉局部平滑、长程趋势和复杂非单调变化，单一基函数难以胜任，因此需要多基函数互补。

Method: 先用移动最小二乘(MLS)为多个互补基函数族估计系数，再用融合权重组合各预测器。提出两种策略：HybridFF (Fixed) 在小集合上校准模型专用的固定融合权重，优先效率；HybridFF (Adaptive) 用全步预测误差的指数移动平均计算分支可靠性分数，在线更新融合权重，提升预测保真度。

Result: 在DiT-XL/2、FLUX.1-dev、SD3.5-Large和HunyuanVideo上，相比代表性单基预测器和缓存基线，展现出更优的加速-质量权衡。

Conclusion: HybridFF作为一种免训练、即插即用的框架，通过多基函数融合有效加速扩散采样，并能在激进缓存下保持生成质量与显著加速。

Abstract: We propose Hybrid-Basis Feature Forecasting (HybridFF), a training-free, plug-and-play framework for accelerating diffusion sampling. To capture local smoothness, long-range trends, and complex non-monotonic variations when modeling feature evolution, HybridFF first estimates coefficients using moving least squares (MLS) for each of multiple complementary basis families and then combines the corresponding predictors using fusion weights. In addition to the choice of basis functions, the fusion weights also play a critical role. We introduce two strategies to balance quality and speedup. HybridFF (Fixed) prioritizes efficiency with model-specific fusion weights calibrated on a small set and held constant during inference. HybridFF (Adaptive) updates the fusion weights online using branch reliability scores computed from an exponential moving average of full-step prediction errors, improving prediction fidelity and generation quality under aggressive caching while retaining substantial acceleration. Experiments across DiT-XL/2, FLUX.1-dev, SD3.5-Large, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate a favorable speedup--quality trade-off over representative single-basis forecasters and caching baselines.

</details>


### [117] [When and What to Prune? Stage-Aware Visual Token Pruning for Efficient VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05273)
*Tianjun Shi,Haotian Xiong,Ziyu Gong,Qi Lu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAPrune，一种免训练的视觉token剪枝框架，用于高效VLA推理。它通过校准集观察动作-视觉注意力跨层变化，在注意力模式可靠后再选择剪枝层，并用双路径规则保护强注意力token及周边上下文。实验显示剪掉87.5%视觉token，推理最高加速1.718倍，任务成功率有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法多采用固定剪枝计划（在预设层剪一次或均匀间隔层剪枝），这对VLA模型有风险：早期层模型可能尚不清楚哪些视觉区域对动作重要，看似不重要的token在结合视觉观察与语言指令后可能变得有用。

Method: SAPrune为免训练框架。它不固定剪枝层，而是用小型校准集观察动作到视觉的注意力如何跨层变化，仅在注意力模式变得可靠后才选择剪枝层。在每个选定的层，应用双路径剪枝规则：一条路径保护被强关注的视觉token不被剪掉，另一条路径防止有用的周边上下文被丢弃。

Result: 在LIBERO、SIMPLER及真实机器人任务上，SAPrune剪除87.5%的视觉token，实现最高1.718倍推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的任务成功率。

Conclusion: SAPrune通过基于注意力可靠性的自适应剪枝层选择和双路径保护规则，在免训练条件下显著减少VLA模型的视觉token，实现高效推理而不明显损失任务性能。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is an effective way to accelerate vision-language models and is especially useful for vision-language-action (VLA) inference, where many visual tokens must be processed before predicting robot actions. Existing pruning methods usually estimate which tokens can be pruned based on attention scores or feature diversity, retaining tokens that are either highly attended or visually different from others. However, most of them use fixed pruning schedules, such as pruning once at a preset layer or pruning at uniformly spaced layers. Such schedules can be risky for VLA models, because the model may not know which visual regions matter for the action in early layers. Tokens that look unimportant at first may become useful after the model combines visual observations with the language instruction. In this work, we propose SAPrune, a training-free visual token pruning framework for efficient VLA inference. Instead of pruning at fixed layers, SAPrune uses a small calibration set to observe how action-to-visual attention changes across layers, and chooses pruning layers only after the attention pattern becomes more reliable. At each selected layer, SAPrune applies a dual-path pruning rule: one path protects strongly attended visual tokens from pruning, while the other prevents useful surrounding context from being discarded. Experiments on LIBERO, SIMPLER, and real-world robotic tasks show that SAPrune prunes 87.5% of visual tokens and achieves up to 1.718x inference speedup while maintaining competitive task success rates.

</details>


### [118] [Answer with Evidence: Consistency-Aware Grounded Visual Question Answering for Roadside Traffic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05274)
*Runwei Guan,Rongsheng Hu,Shangshu Chen,Ningwei Ouyang,Shaofeng Liang,Heyi Lin,Jinjing Zhu,Yang Shi,Dongming Wu,Daizong Liu,Henghui Ding,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对路面交通推理中的"说-指不一致"问题，提出先枚举后回答（EtA）框架及ECPO强化学习策略，并构建RoadSceneVQA-G基准与AGC评价体系，显著提升答案与框的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 grounded MLLM 存在"说-指不一致"（say-point mismatch）：文本答案与模型定位的边界框相矛盾，而分别评估答案和框的指标无法惩罚此类失败。作者将其根源归结为传统的"先回答后定位"分解方式——在枚举任何物体之前就已确定了数值性结论。

Method: 1）构建 RoadSceneVQA-G 基准，含 34.7K 问答对，每个自由形式答案都关联支撑它的框集合，并提出 Answer-Grounding Consistency（AGC）评测套件；2）提出 Enumerate-then-Answer（EtA），颠倒生成顺序，使答案与证据一致成为输出结构的固有属性；3）提出 Enumeration-Consistent Policy Optimization（ECPO），一种强化学习阶段，以多次 rollout 的并集作为召回教师，无需真值框监督。

Result: EtA 将说-指一致性从 26.6% 提升至 93.7%，grounding F1 从 52.2% 提升至 73.0%；ECPO 在无逐框监督下进一步将 F1 提升至 75.6%。在 gRefCOCO 上，同一框架优于最强对比方法，表明其可迁移至交通场景之外。

Conclusion: 通过逆转生成顺序并引入结构化一致性约束，可将答案-证据一致性内化为输出结构与优化目标的一部分，从而有效缓解说-指不一致，且方法具有跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Roadside traffic reasoning requires every free-form textual claim to be backed by visual evidence. Existing grounded multimodal large language models (MLLMs) frequently exhibit say-point mismatch, in which the textual answer contradicts the bounding boxes the model localizes. Evaluation metrics that score answers and boxes separately leave this failure unpenalized. We trace the mismatch to the conventional answer-then-ground factorization, which commits to a numerical claim before any object is enumerated. To measure it, we build RoadSceneVQA-G, a benchmark of 34.7K question-answer pairs in which every free-form answer is linked to the set of boxes that witnesses it, and we propose the Answer-Grounding Consistency (AGC) evaluation suite. To address it, we introduce Enumerate-then-Answer (EtA), which reverses the generation order so that answer-evidence agreement becomes a property of the output structure, and Enumeration-Consistent Policy Optimization (ECPO), a reinforcement learning stage that uses the union of multiple rollouts as a recall teacher without ground-truth boxes. EtA raises say-point consistency from 26.6\% to 93.7\% and grounding F1 from 52.2\% to 73.0\%, and ECPO further increases F1 to 75.6\% without per-box supervision. On gRefCOCO, the same framework outperforms the strongest compared method, indicating that it transfers beyond traffic scenes. The project is available at \url{https://github.com/GuanRunwei/RoadSceneVQA-G}.

</details>


### [119] [Mobile-4DGS: Unified Static-Dynamic Real-time Mobile Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05289)
*Xiaobiao Du,Beixi Hao,Zhen Fang,Tianqing Zhu,Richard Hartley,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mobile-4DGS：面向移动端静态与动态高斯渲染的统一轻量框架，通过SH压缩、剪枝策略与显式4D表示实现实时高保真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在新视角合成上表现优异，但在资源受限的移动设备上部署静态与动态高斯表示仍面临存储开销大、图元冗余、逐帧计算昂贵等问题。

Method: 1) 提出蒙特卡洛镜面能量聚合器，将高阶辐射残差压缩至一阶球谐；2) 设计属性条件SH增强模块，预测偏移在推理前预烘焙；3) 提出基于多视角Alpha的致密化与剪枝策略抑制冗余图元；4) 对动态场景构建紧凑显式4D表示（二阶高斯运动、可学习时间支撑、静态-动态二值划分），无需运行时形变网络；5) 基于该划分设计深度顺序证书，选择性复用已提交的深度顺序以减少重投影、排序、合并与索引缓冲更新。

Result: 在静态与动态场景的大量实验中，Mobile-4DGS显著降低存储与渲染开销，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量，实现移动设备上的实时3D与4D高斯渲染。

Conclusion: 该框架为移动平台上静态与动态高斯表示的轻量化、实时化部署提供了统一解决方案，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable performance in novel view synthesis, yet deploying both static and dynamic Gaussian representations on resource-constrained mobile devices remains challenging due to heavy storage, redundant primitives, and costly per-frame computation. We present Mobile-4DGS, a unified lightweight framework for high-fidelity real-time static and dynamic Gaussian rendering on mobile platforms. For compact appearance modeling, we introduce a Monte Carlo Specular Energy Aggregator that compresses high-order radiance residuals into the first-order Spherical Harmonics (SH), together with an Attribute-Conditioned SH Enhancement module whose predicted offsets are pre-baked before inference. We further propose a Multi-View Alpha-Based Densification and Pruning strategy to suppress redundant primitives while maintaining multi-view consistency. For dynamic scenes, we develop a compact explicit 4D representation by constructing second-order Gaussian motion, learnable temporal support, and a binary static-dynamic partition, enabling continuous-time modeling without runtime deformation networks. Based on this partition, a Depth-Order Certificate selectively reuses previously committed depth orders to reduce re-projection, sorting, merging, and index-buffer updates during playback. Extensive experiments on static and dynamic scenes demonstrate that Mobile-4DGS substantially reduces storage and rendering overhead while maintaining competitive visual quality, enabling real-time 3D and 4D Gaussian Splatting on mobile devices. \textcolor{magenta}{\href{https://xiaobiaodu.github.io/mobile-4dgs-project/}{Code has been released: https://xiaobiaodu.github.io/mobile-4dgs-project/}}.

</details>


### [120] [BossouChimpanzee: Long-term Chimpanzee Video Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05293)
*Daniel Schofield,Susana Carvalho,Vladimir Iashin,Andrew Zisserman,Max Bain,Arsha Nagrani,David Ng,Claudia Sousa,Boniface Zogbila,Jules Doré,Dora Biro,Misato Hayashi,Tetsuro Matsuzawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍BossouChimpanzee视频数据集：记录几内亚Bossou野生黑猩猩三十年（1988-2018）超1200小时连续视频，并先行发布约74小时的BossouChimpanzee70h快照，含23个已识别个体，聚焦个体与动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为动物行为学与认知研究提供长期、连续的野外视听记录资源，同时为机器学习模型在野外视听数据上的训练与评估提供基准。

Method: 依托几内亚Bossou户外实验站的协作野外工作，长期采集并整理黑猩猩实验范式的连续视频档案；给出主数据集的关键统计与结构说明，并构建初始约74小时、覆盖23个已识别个体的快照子集，围绕个体识别与动作识别任务组织数据。

Result: 形成跨越三十年、超1200小时的黑猩猩连续视频档案；已发布BossouChimpanzee70h约74小时快照，包含23个可识别个体，可用于个体与动作识别的基准任务。

Conclusion: 该数据集是行为学与认知研究的重要资源，也是野外视听机器学习研究的丰富基准，后续将在完整视频资源发布前先行开放部分数据。

Abstract: We describe the BossouChimpanzee video dataset, a unique long-term visual record of wild chimpanzees at an outdoor laboratory for field experiments in Bossou, Guinea, spanning three decades (1988-2018) and comprising over 1,200 hours of continuous video recordings collected through collaborative fieldwork and research. In this paper, we outline the history and scientific contributions of the experimental paradigm and video archive, provide key statistics and details on the structure of the main video dataset, and release an initial ~74h snapshot, BossouChimpanzee70h, covering 23 identified individuals focused on chimpanzee individual and action recognition, ahead of the full video resource. This dataset represents a valuable resource for cognitive and behavioural research in ethology and a rich benchmark for training and evaluating machine learning models on audiovisual data from the wild.

</details>


### [121] [Revisiting Ground-Truth Synthesis from High-Speed Video: Exact Validity Conditions and an Audited Consumer Capture Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05298)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Sumaiya Rahim Suma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文分析了运动去模糊数据集合成中标签偏差的来源，发现中心帧标签只有在窗口为奇数且各帧采样时长相等时才是无偏的，否则会因奇偶性和时序表记录的非等长/非标称帧率而产生系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动去模糊数据集通常通过平均N帧高帧率连续帧并以中央帧作为标签来合成，但作者质疑这一标签是否在所有拍摄时序下都成立，尤其在实际采集帧率与标称值不符、采样时长不等或窗口为偶数时可能引入误导性监督信号。

Method: 作者从理论上推导了平均帧与中央帧标签之间对齐偏差的条件，分析了奇偶窗口和不等采样时长的影响；随后审计了51段标称240 fps的智能手机视频片段，通过容器的时序表检查真实帧率，并在受控实验中比较奇偶选择与这些时序不规则性对拟合线性去模糊滤波器的影响，同时区分模糊标签与插值标签的可修复性。

Result: 理论表明偶数窗口会在完美时序下仍使每个标签偏移固定的模糊长度比例；不等采样时长的平均错位为零，数据集统计无法揭示，但会在无法从模糊中读出偏移时对监督信号进行卷积而非加噪。审计发现51段片段中有19段实际约为176 fps，仅记录在容器的时序表中。受控实验中奇偶选择对拟合线性去模糊滤波器的损失远大于这些时序不规则性，而插值标签可借助真实帧时间修复，模糊标签则不能。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了运动去模糊数据集构建中被忽视的标签偏差问题，提供了一个无需解码即可检查奇偶窗口和采样时长两种条件的工具，并将发布相关片段及其时序表，以促进更可靠的去模糊监督和数据集构建。

Abstract: Motion-deblurring datasets are commonly synthesised by averaging $N$ consecutive high-frame-rate frames and labelling the result with the central frame. We show that this label is unbiased for every capture timing only when the window is odd and the frames' sample durations are equal. An even window shifts every label by a fixed fraction of the blur length, even under perfect timing. Unequal durations are subtler: their mean misalignment is zero, so dataset statistics cannot reveal them, yet when the offset cannot be read from the blur they convolve the supervision rather than adding noise to it. Auditing 51 smartphone clips recorded at a nominal 240 fps, we find 19 captured near 176 fps, a behaviour recorded only in the container's timing tables. In a controlled test the parity choice costs a fitted linear deblurring filter far more than these timing irregularities do, and interpolation labels, unlike blur labels, can be repaired with the true frame times. We provide a tool that checks both conditions without decoding, and will release the clips and their timing tables.

</details>


### [122] [Kinematics-Centric Continuous Sign Language Retrieval with Gloss-Guided Boundary-Aware Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05306)
*Chang Liu,Ke Han,Davide Talon,Elisa Ricci,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以运动学为中心的手语-文本对齐框架，采用3D SMPL-X运动表示替代RGB外观表示，结合gloss引导的局部对齐机制与视觉蒸馏策略，在CSL-Daily和PHOENIX-2014T上取得优异检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语-文本对齐方法主要依赖以RGB外观为主的表示，将运动语义与视觉变化纠缠在一起，导致动作-语言对齐模糊。手语-文本对齐是文本驱动手语理解的基础性挑战，需要更结构化、语义一致的表征方式。

Method: 1) 将手语-文本对齐重构到结构化运动学空间，以3D SMPL-X运动为主要表示，在统一运动空间中显式建模手语运动动力学，减少对外观信号的依赖；2) 引入gloss引导的局部对齐机制，利用gloss时间跨度作为弱监督，将连续动作分解为连贯片段，建立细粒度动作-文本对应；3) 开发视觉蒸馏策略，训练时将RGB信号作为特权监督提供互补上下文线索，推理时完全移除RGB。

Result: 在标准基准上，方法在CSL-Daily上取得最先进的双向检索性能，在PHOENIX-2014T上取得具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 结果验证了运动学表示与显式局部对齐对手语-文本对齐的有效性，表明结构化运动表示和细粒度局部grounding是提升手语理解的重要方向。

Abstract: Sign language-text alignment remains a fundamental challenge for text-driven sign language understanding. Existing methods predominantly rely on appearance-heavy RGB representations, which entangle motion semantics with visual variations and lead to ambiguous motion-language grounding. In this paper, we reformulate sign language-text alignment in a structured kinematic space and propose a kinematics-centric framework that adopts 3D SMPL-X motion as the primary representation. By explicitly modeling the kinematic dynamics of signing in a unified motion space, our approach reduces reliance on appearance signals and yields more semantically consistent representations. To capture the compositional nature of sign language, we introduce a gloss-guided local alignment mechanism that leverages gloss temporal spans as weak supervision to decompose continuous motion into coherent segments and establish fine-grained motion-text correspondences, thereby reducing ambiguity in localizing word-level semantics in continuous signing. Furthermore, we develop a visual distillation strategy, where RGB signals serve as privileged supervision during training to provide complementary contextual cues, while being completely removed at inference time. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art bidirectional retrieval performance on CSL-Daily and competitive results on PHOENIX-2014T. These results highlight the effectiveness of kinematic representations and explicit local grounding for sign language-text alignment.

</details>


### [123] [Shadow Feature Refinement Network: Progressive Feature Refinement based on Knowledge Distillation for Effective Shadow Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05325)
*Donghyun Han,Byoung-Dai Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFR-Net，结合监督学习、特征细化损失与知识蒸馏，并加入后处理算法，以提升阴影去除性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中阴影去除仍具挑战，因阴影尺寸和颜色受光照条件影响而变化。

Method: 提出阴影特征细化网络SFR-Net，利用监督学习、特征细化损失和知识蒸馏；引入专用后处理算法恢复无阴影图像的自然色彩一致性。

Result: 在ISTD+上RMSE为3.4627、SSIM为0.9382；在SRD上RMSE为4.3781、SSIM为0.9341，在阴影与非阴影区域均表现有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法在多样条件下泛化能力强，为稳健且感知自然的阴影去除提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: In the field of deep learning, has seen significant advancements; however, shadow removal remains a persistent challenge owing to the variable sizes and colors of shadows influenced by lighting conditions. This study proposes a novel shadow feature refinement network (SFR-Net), which leverages supervised learning, feature refinement loss, and knowledge distillation to enhance shadow removal performance. A dedicated post-processing algorithm is further introduced to restore natural color consistency in the generated shadow-free images. We evaluated our method on two public datasets: the adjusted image shadow triplet dataset (ISTD+) and the shadow removal dataset (SRD), which demonstrate strong generalization capabilities under diverse conditions. On ISTD+, our model achieved a root mean square error (RMSE) of 3.4627 and structural similarity index measure (SSIM) of 0.9382 across the entire image. On SRD, it recorded an RMSE of 4.3781 and an SSIM of 0.9341. These comprehensive results show that our approach performs competitively across both shadow and non-shadow regions while setting a promising direction for robust and perceptually natural shadow removal. Code is available at https://github.com/DongHyun99/SFRNet.

</details>


### [124] [Riemannian Shape Analysis of the Corpus Callosum in Kendall Space: Aging and Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05326)
*Olakunle S. Abawonse,Fatou Fall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Kendall形状空间的黎曼形状空间框架，对OASIS-1队列中矢状面胼胝体轮廓进行测地回归分析，发现年龄相关形变以后部（Splenium与Isthmus）为主，且在阿尔茨海默病中该轨迹被打乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胼胝体是脑衰老的重要白质标志物，但多数研究用标量摘要量化它，丢弃了其边界几何信息。作者希望用保留完整轮廓几何的黎曼形状空间方法，更精确地刻画年龄相关的形态变化并定位到具体解剖亚区。

Method: 将每条中矢状面胼胝体轮廓用128个地标表示，嵌入Kendall形状空间（去除平移、旋转、尺度）。推导带精确黎曼梯度的多元测地回归，并用拟合的年龄-速度场将形变定位到五个解剖亚区。应用于OASIS-1队列，对比认知正常组与阿尔茨海默病组。

Result: 认知正常组（n=252）中测地回归优于欧氏线性基准（R²=0.1355 vs 0.1216）。区域能量以后部为主：Splenium占41.4%，Isthmus占23.8%，合计约65%，但仅占约35%的地标。符号投影确认该排序（Splenium r=0.570；Isthmus r=0.473）。阿尔茨海默病组（n=88）中年龄仅解释不到0.5%的形状方差。切空间分类器仅用2D轮廓即达到年龄组AUC=0.791，超过近期体积基准（0.67）。

Conclusion: 保留轮廓几何的黎曼形状空间测地回归能更有效捕捉胼胝体的年龄相关形变，并定位到后部亚区；该健康衰老轨迹在阿尔茨海默病中被显著扰乱，且简单2D轮廓即可提供优于体积方法的年龄分类性能。

Abstract: The corpus callosum (CC) is a major white-matter structure and a well-established marker of brain aging, but most studies quantify it using scalar summaries that discard its boundary geometry. We present a Riemannian shape-space framework for analyzing age-related morphological change in the midsagittal CC, applied to the OASIS-1 cohort. Each contour is represented by $128$ landmarks and embedded into Kendall shape space, where translation, rotation, and scale are removed. We derive a multivariate geodesic regression with exact Riemannian gradients and use the fitted age-velocity field to localize age-related deformation to five anatomical sub-regions. In the cognitively normal cohort ($n = 252$), geodesic regression outperforms the Euclidean linear benchmark ($R^2 = 0.1355$ vs.\ $0.1216$). Regional energy is posterior-dominant: the Splenium carries $41.4\%$ and the Isthmus $23.8\%$ of total age-related shape change, together accounting for $\sim 65\%$ despite comprising only $\sim 35\%$ of landmarks. Signed projections confirm the ordering (Splenium $r = 0.570$; Isthmus $r = 0.473$). In contrast, age explains less than $0.5\%$ of shape variance in Alzheimer's disease ($n = 88$), indicating that the disease disrupts the healthy aging trajectory. A tangent-space classifier achieves an age-group AUC of $0.791$ from the 2D contour alone, exceeding a recent volumetric benchmark ($0.67$).

</details>


### [125] [WILLIE: A Unified Framework and Benchmark for Wound Classification, Segmentation, and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05341)
*Gopi Trinadh Maddikunta,Shannan Hamlin,Hsin-Mei Chen,Kimaya Barnes,Peizhu Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出WILLIE统一框架与基准，将伤口分类、分割与定位整合到单一模型中，在统一协议下系统评估多任务伤口分析，最佳模型单次前向即达91.88%分类准确率、91.41% Dice和96.23% AP@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国慢性伤口患者超过820万，临床与经济负担沉重。临床伤口评估涉及分类、边界勾画与区域定位三个相互耦合的任务，但现有机器学习方法通常用独立模型分别处理，缺乏统一的评估协议与系统性对比。

Method: 提出WILLIE统一框架与基准：将三个公开伤口数据集协调统一为共享基准；在三种缩放配置下比较统一模型，并与10个单任务基线进行对比。

Result: 最佳模型单次前向传播即同时输出三种结果，达到91.88%分类准确率、91.41% Dice与96.23% AP@0.5；此外，基于分割导出的定位在该基准上优于专用检测基线。

Conclusion: 基于分割的定位可能使基于边界框的定位变得不必要，因为伤口目标在空间上具有连贯性。医疗影像中有效的多任务学习不仅依赖共享表征，还取决于任务形式化、任务兼容性与基准设计。

Abstract: Chronic wound management affects over 8.2 million patients in the United States and imposes substantial clinical and economic burden. Clinical wound assessment commonly involves three coupled tasks: identifying wound type, delineating wound boundaries, and localizing the wound region for measurement and monitoring. Despite this clinical coupling, existing machine learning approaches typically address wound classification, segmentation, and localization using separate models. We present WILLIE, a unified framework and benchmark for wound classification, segmentation and localization that enables systematic evaluation of multi-task wound analysis under a common protocol. WILLIE harmonizes three public wound datasets into a shared benchmark and compares unified models across three scaling configurations against 10 single-task baselines. The best model achieves 91.88% classification accuracy, 91.41% Dice, and 96.23% AP@0.5 while producing all three outputs in a single forward pass. Beyond aggregate performance, our results show that segmentation-derived localization outperforms dedicated detection baselines in this benchmark, suggesting that box-based localization may be unnecessary for spatially coherent wound targets. Our findings highlight that effective multi-task learning in healthcare imaging depends not only on shared representations, but also on task formulation, compatibility, and benchmark design.

</details>


### [126] [IRSTD-Agent: Agentic Infrared Small Target Detection via Zoom-Guided Interaction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05342)
*Jiawen Xi,Yu Zhang,Tianyi Zhao,Zhu Liu,Maoxun Yuan,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRSTD-Agent，一个基于多模态大语言模型的智能体框架，通过动态视觉搜索实现红外小目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测在海上监测和空中侦察中很重要，但现有基于MLLM的方法难以精确定位红外小目标。

Method: 框架让MLLM自适应决定检查图像的位置和尺度，逐步收集细粒度视觉证据。采用五个互补视觉工具（PROPOSAL、ZOOM、DETECT、DROP、REFINE）支持候选发现、自适应观察、目标定位、假设排除和目标范围细化。并提出缩放引导交互学习，用标注衍生的交互轨迹监督工具选择及参数。

Result: 在WideIRSTD-Full和IRSTD-1k数据集上，IRSTD-Agent优于所评估的视觉语言模型，并提升了MLLM在IRSTD任务中的精确定位能力。

Conclusion: IRSTD-Agent通过智能体式动态视觉搜索有效增强了MLLM对红外小目标的精确定位能力。

Abstract: Infrared small-target detection plays an important role in maritime monitoring and aerial surveillance. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer promising capabilities for visual understanding, existing MLLM-based approaches struggle to precisely localize infrared small targets. In this paper, we propose IRSTD-Agent, an agentic framework for infrared small target detection through dynamic visual search. The framework enables an MLLM to adaptively determine where and at what scale to inspect an image and progressively gather fine-grained visual evidence for precise target localization. Five complementary visual tools (PROPOSAL, ZOOM, DETECT, DROP and REFINE) support object candidate discovery, adaptive observation, target localization, hypothesis rejection, and target extent refinement, together enabling a coordinated search process over original-resolution images. To teach the MLLMs to conduct this search, we introduce Zoom-guided Interaction Learning, which uses annotation-derived interaction trajectories to supervise tool selection and the corresponding arguments. Through extensive experiments on WideIRSTD-Full and IRSTD-1k datasets, we demonstrate that IRSTD-Agent outperforms the evaluated vision-language models and enhances the precise localization capabilities of MLLMs in IRSTD tasks.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Conditional Source Distribution via Flow Reversal for Temporal Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05349)
*Kuan-Hsun Tu,Hsuan-Chi Liu,Jia-Wei Liao,Chien-Sheng Chiang,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CNP-Flow 提出一种基于流反转的流匹配框架，通过学习条件源分布提升时序生成质量，在视频预测、插值和机器人运动规划任务上均有一致改进，并以更少函数评估匹配基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准条件流匹配（FM）仅通过向量场引入条件，源样本始终从标准高斯分布中采样，未能针对不同时序条件定制源分布，限制了生成质量与效率。作者希望让源分布也能随条件变化，从而更贴合时序生成任务的需求。

Method: CNP-Flow 采用条件噪声预测器（CNP）为每个时序条件生成各向同性高斯源分布。CNP 通过流反转获得监督信号：将观测目标经由预训练 FM 模型反向映射回源空间。整体为三阶段流程：预训练 FM 模型、训练 CNP、再用学到的源分布微调 FM 模型，同时保持 FM 主干架构不变。

Result: 在视频预测、视频插值以及 7 自由度 Franka 机器人运动规划三类任务上，CNP-Flow 的生成质量均稳定优于基线；同时以更少的函数评估次数即可达到与基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过在源分布层面引入条件建模，CNP-Flow 有效增强了条件流匹配的时序生成能力，兼顾了生成质量与采样效率，且不改变原有 FM 主干，具有良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce CNP-Flow, a flow matching framework for temporal generation that learns conditional source distributions through flow reversal. Whereas standard conditional flow matching (FM) incorporates conditioning through the vector field and draws source samples from a standard Gaussian, CNP-Flow uses a conditional noise predictor (CNP) to produce an isotropic Gaussian source for each temporal condition. The CNP is supervised by source samples obtained through flow reversal, which maps observed targets backward through a pretrained FM model. A three-stage pipeline pretrains the FM model, trains the CNP, and fine-tunes the FM model using the learned source distribution, while preserving the FM backbone architecture. Across video prediction, video interpolation, and 7-DoF Franka robot motion planning, CNP-Flow consistently improves generation quality. It also matches baseline performance with fewer function evaluations. Project page: https://embodiedai-ntu.github.io/cnpflow

</details>


### [128] [CleanMDM: Clean Motion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Motion Cleanup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05411)
*Zhe Li,Shicheng Wang,Bowen Cai,Huan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CleanMDM是一个统一的多模态运动清理框架，将清理任务建模为带即插即用条件的掩码条件生成，支持含噪3D运动、稀疏2D/3D关键帧和文本的任意组合，实现自动与可控清理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动捕捉数据常存在缺失片段、抖动、漂移和接触伪影，难以直接使用。传统上依赖动画师手动识别关键帧、修正并插值，成本高。生成式运动模型虽可实现自动清理，但多为黑盒去噪器，可控性差，难以保留可靠片段或满足用户特定意图。受动画工作流启发，作者希望构建兼具自动性与可控性的清理框架。

Method: 提出CleanMDM统一多模态清理框架，将清理表述为带即插即用条件的掩码条件生成，单一模型支持含噪3D运动、稀疏2D关键帧、稀疏3D关键帧与文本的任意组合。为进一步提升真实感，引入Latent Motion Quality Discriminator (LMQD)以更好匹配运动学分布，减少滑步、抖动与穿插伪影；并采用Mesh-Aware Contact Projection作为测试时优化步骤，增强接触与物理一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，CleanMDM持续优于先前的清理与生成基线；低成本的文本与2D关键帧条件在多模态清理场景中带来可靠的可控性提升。

Conclusion: CleanMDM将动画工作流思想引入运动清理，通过统一的多模态条件生成框架，同时实现无需额外标注的自动清理与多模态引导下的可控清理，并结合LMQD与Mesh-Aware Contact Projection提升真实感与物理一致性，验证了低成本条件在可控性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Motion capture data is rarely directly usable, as they typically exhibit missing segments, jitter, drift and contact artifacts. Traditionally, corrupted motions are cleaned by animators through the manual identification of keyframes from noisy motion, subsequent keyframe correction, and interpolation between corrected keyframes to reconstruct coherent motion. While the rise of generative motion models has made automatic cleanup feasible, most approaches operate as black box denoisers with limited controllability, making it difficult to preserve reliable segments or enforce specific user intents. Inspired by animation workflows, we present CleanMDM, a unified multimodal motion cleanup framework that formulates cleanup as masked conditional generation with plug-and-play conditions. This single model supports arbitrary combinations of noisy 3D motion, sparse 2D keyframes, sparse 3D keyframes, and text. This design enables both automatic cleanup without additional user annotation and controllable cleanup under multimodal guidance. To further improve motion realism, we incorporate the Latent Motion Quality Discriminator (LMQD) to better match kinematic distributions and reduce skating, jitter, and interpenetration artifacts, and we apply Mesh-Aware Contact Projection as a test-time optimization step to enhance contact and physical consistency. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that CleanMDM consistently outperforms prior cleanup and generation baselines, and that low cost conditions (text and 2D keyframes) provide reliable controllability gains in multimodal cleanup scenarios.

</details>


### [129] [A Strong Baseline for Evaluating Vision Encoders in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05413)
*Yilin Yang,Jun-Tao Tang,Kengyi Wang,Siyuan Su,Gaoyong Luo,Mingda Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视视觉编码器的跨模态评估方法，指出前人工作在实验设计与方法公式化上的局限，并提出无需训练的RAVEL方法，基于跨模态最近邻检索，在实验中取得显著领先的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视觉编码器需要能可靠预测其在多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中下游性能的指标。尽管跨模态指标被证明更能捕捉这种性能，但实践中单模态指标仍占主导地位，因此有必要重新审视跨模态评估的有效性。

Method: 通过大规模实验重新审视视觉编码器的跨模态评估，识别先前方法在实验设计与方法公式化上的重要局限，并在解决这些局限、引入简单改进的基础上，提出无需训练的RAVEL方法，其核心为跨模态最近邻检索。

Result: 尽管RAVEL方法简单，但在所有实验中均达到最先进水平，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 在严谨、全面的评估设置下，简单的跨模态指标可为评估面向MLLM的视觉编码器提供强有力的基础。

Abstract: Evaluating vision encoders requires metrics that reliably predict their downstream performance in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Although recent studies have shown that cross-modal metrics can better capture such performance, unimodal metrics remain the dominant choice in practice. In this work, we revisit cross-modal evaluation of vision encoders through large-scale experiments. We identify important limitations in both the experimental design and methodological formulation of prior approaches. After addressing these limitations and introducing simple improvements, we propose RAVEL, a training-free method based on cross-modal nearest-neighbor retrieval. Despite its simplicity, RAVEL achieves state-of-the-art performance across our experiments, outperforming prior methods by a substantial margin. Our results demonstrate that simple cross-modal metrics, when evaluated under a careful and comprehensive setup, can provide a strong basis for evaluating vision encoders for MLLMs.

</details>


### [130] [Prism: Dynamic Sparse Attention for Native 2K Joint Video-Audio Generation Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05416)
*Shuyuan Tu,Qi Tian,Yinming Huang,Yue Wu,Xintong Han,Kaihang Pan,Weijie Kong,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jian-Wei Zhang,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Prism，一种用于原生训练2K分辨率联合视频-音频生成模型的动态稀疏注意力框架，通过自适应块划分和混合块选择策略，实现2.5倍训练加速并超越全注意力的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更高分辨率下原生训练联合视频-音频生成模型能学习更丰富的视觉细节和更锐利的运动动态，但全注意力存在二次计算成本，且随着分辨率提升，注意力会分散到越来越多冗余token上，稀释了有效内容的学习信号并破坏预训练先验。现有稀疏注意力方法要么面向免训练加速，要么忽视了联合视频-音频数据的独特结构——跨模态交互本质上集中在发声区域附近。

Method: Prism将token序列组织为时空宏区域（macro-zones），使注意力结构能自适应局部内容。对每个区域，它通过视频特征沿通道的方差以及音频到视频的交叉注意力特征范数，估计局部信息结构，联合捕捉视觉内容的方向性变化和音频对各视觉区域的影响强度。基于这些信号，Prism为每个区域动态分配定制的块形状，在视觉内容快速变化的轴和音视频耦合强的轴上应用更精细的划分，使块内token保持语义连贯，从而让块级特征同时捕捉视觉内容和音视频交互模式。此外，Prism采用混合块选择策略动态确定每个查询的稀疏度。

Result: 实验表明，Prism相比全注意力实现了2.5倍训练加速，同时在生成质量上超越全注意力。

Conclusion: Prism通过面向联合视频-音频数据结构的动态稀疏注意力设计，在2K分辨率原生训练中兼顾了训练效率与生成质量，验证了利用跨模态交互集中性来指导稀疏注意力划分的有效性。

Abstract: Natively training joint video-audio generation models at higher resolutions empowers them to learn richer visual details and sharper motion dynamics. However, full attention incurs quadratic cost and, as resolution increases, spreads attention over increasingly redundant tokens, diluting learning signals for informative content and disrupting pretrained priors. Existing sparse attention methods either target training-free acceleration or overlook the unique structure of joint video-audio data, where cross-modal interactions are inherently concentrated around sound-producing regions. To address this, we propose Prism, a dynamic sparse attention framework for natively training joint video-audio generation models at 2K. In particular, Prism organizes the token sequence into spatiotemporal macro-zones, enabling the attention structure to adapt to local content. For each zone, it estimates local information structure via video feature variance along the channel and feature norms from the audio-to-video cross-attention, jointly capturing how visual content varies directionally and how strongly audio influences each visual region. Based on these signals, Prism dynamically assigns a tailored block shape to each zone, applying finer partitioning along axes of rapid visual content variation and strong audio-visual coupling. This encourages tokens within each block to remain semantically coherent, allowing block-level features to capture both visual content and joint video-audio interaction patterns. Prism further adopts a hybrid block selection strategy to dynamically determine per-query sparsity. Experiments show that Prism achieves 2.5$\times$ training speedup compared to full attention, while surpassing it in generation quality.

</details>


### [131] [Render to Reason: Novel-View Semantic Prediction Improves Spatial Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05417)
*Yuqun Wu,Yao Xiao,Chuhang Zou,Shenlong Wang,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现将预训练3D几何特征融入视觉语言模型效果有限，原因是标准空间问答可从视觉与语言先验中作答，导致几何通路梯度微弱。作者提出"新视角语义渲染"辅助训练任务以强化几何通路，在三个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作尝试用预训练3D模型为视觉语言模型增强几何特征，期望提升空间推理能力。但作者发现，仅将几何特征与视觉特征融合并在标准空间问答上训练，对高层多跳任务仅有边际提升，因此需要找出瓶颈并提出改进方案。

Method: 作者将问题归因于训练信号不足：标准空间问答大多可依靠视觉特征和语言先验回答，几何通路获得的梯度较弱，难以与视觉特征有效整合。为此提出"新视角语义渲染"作为辅助训练任务，要求模型预测未观测视角的语义布局，受人类心理模拟新视角能力的启发。该任务强制两条通路协同：几何提供与姿态相关的可见性，视觉提供语义内容。

Result: 相比几何增强基线，该辅助任务在三个基准上均取得一致提升：VSI-Bench上最高+1.6，ReVSI上+2.2，自建3D-Point-QA数据集上+2.9；完整模型在VSI-Bench和ReVSI上超过此前的开源方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入需要几何信息的辅助训练信号，可有效促进几何与视觉通路的联合利用，从而提升视觉语言模型的高层空间推理能力。

Abstract: Recent works augment Vision-Language Models with geometry features from pretrained 3D models, expecting that the geometric signal will boost spatial reasoning. However, we find that simply fusing geometry features and training on standard spatial QA yields only marginal improvements on high-level multi-hop tasks. We attribute this gap to a training-signal problem: standard spatial QA can be largely answered from visual features and language priors, so the geometry pathway receives weak gradients and fails to integrate with the visual features. To provide a training signal that requires geometry, we propose \textbf{novel-view semantic rendering} as an auxiliary training task that requires the model to predict the semantic layout of an unobserved viewpoint, inspired by humans' ability to mentally simulate novel viewpoints during spatial reasoning. This task encourages joint use of both pathways: geometry provides pose-dependent visibility, while vision provides semantic content. Our auxiliary task yields consistent improvements over the geometry-augmented baseline across all three benchmarks (up to +1.6 on VSI-Bench, +2.2 on ReVSI, +2.9 on our 3D-Point-QA dataset) and our full model surpasses prior open-source methods on VSI-Bench and on ReVSI. Project page: https://yuqunw.github.io/Render2Reason/.

</details>


### [132] [Unmentioned Checklist Findings Change How Reinforcement Learning Appears to Improve Chest Radiograph Report Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05425)
*Ali Vosoughi,Akhil Kasturi,Chenliang Xu,Axel Wismueller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用强化学习训练视觉语言模型，从胸片生成12项发现的检查清单，再由独立检查模型对报告句子做自动核查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射报告自动核查若依赖AI生成清单，可能遗漏未提及的发现，需要评估该范式下核查模型的判别能力与可靠性。

Method: 强化学习训练视觉语言模型在未见测试句的情况下填写12项发现清单；另设检查模型依据清单判断句子。采用规则化核查与独立医学核查者（均未参与训练）测量判别增益（Youden指数），并比较训练格式（固定发现顺序、未提及项记为阴性）的影响，另在8个检查模型上测试对未提及发现阴性陈述的接受度。

Result: 在留出患者上，规则化核查与独立医学核查者的判别增益分别为12.6%和11.8%，仅规则化核查满足预设假警报标准。改用训练格式后，训练检查模型增益升高、独立检查者降低，预设比较为6.2%（95%区间2.0%–10.5%），事后在留出患者上为7.7%。8个检查模型对未提及发现阴性陈述的接受度为1.0%至97.0%。标签源自报告，未经放射科医生裁定。

Conclusion: AI生成清单下的自动核查可行且具判别增益，但性能高度依赖格式与检查模型，未提及发现的阴性陈述接受度差异极大，且标签未经专家裁定，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Automated checks of radiology reports may rely on AI-generated checklists that leave findings unmentioned. We used reinforcement learning to train a vision-language model to fill in a 12-finding checklist from a chest radiograph without seeing the sentence under test; a separate checking model judged the sentence from the checklist. On held-out patients, a rule-based check and an independent medical checker, neither used in training, measured discrimination gains (Youden index) of 12.6% and 11.8%; only the rule-based check met the prespecified false-alarm criterion. Switching to the training format, which fixes finding order and enters unmentioned findings as absent, raised the training checker's measured gain and lowered the independent checker's, a prespecified comparison that yielded 6.2% (95% interval 2.0% to 10.5%) and, post hoc on held-out patients, 7.7%. Across 8 checking models, acceptance of a label-consistent negative statement about an unmentioned finding ranged from 1.0% to 97.0%. Labels were report-derived, not radiologist-adjudicated.

</details>


### [133] [DynaMesh: Dynamic 3D Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05529)
*Raj Hansini,Guan Chen,Rana Hanocka,Itai Lang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaMesh 提出一种针对 3D 网格的动态纹理生成方法，在几何不变的前提下生成随时间演化的纹理外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态 3D 生成多聚焦于运动（几何变化、外观不变），而纹理生成则把外观当作固定表面属性，二者都无法处理在 3D 物体上传播的视觉效果。直接用视频模型加逐帧 image-to-3D 会带来闪烁、细节丢失和网格不一致问题。

Method: 以网格渲染图与文本提示为条件驱动视频模型得到参考视频，再用冻结的 image-to-3D 生成器处理视频；将每帧条件在时间窗口上混合，并为每个形状拟合低秩适配器以恢复细节；网格仅编码一次，从而保证几何恒定，输出为单一网格加每帧纹理。

Result: 在多种物体和效果上，DynaMesh 显著优于近期 video-to-4D 与纹理化方法，并能将时间效果泛化到训练中未见过的不同形状。

Conclusion: DynaMesh 通过条件混合与低秩适配解决了逐帧生成的不一致问题，实现了几何固定、纹理随时间演化的动态 3D 外观生成。

Abstract: We present DynaMesh, a dynamic texture generation method for 3D meshes. Given a textureless shape and a text prompt describing an effect, our method produces an appearance that evolves while the object's geometry remains unchanged. Previous works on dynamic 3D content generation have focused on motion, where an object's geometry and position change while keeping its appearance the same. Methods on texture generation sit on the other side of the problem, painting appearance onto a shape as a fixed surface property and not as an evolving process. Neither addresses a visual effect that propagates on a 3D object. A natural route consists of two generators: a video model that shows the effect from a single view, and an image-to-3D generator that lifts each frame to 3D. However, the latter has no notion of time, so running it per video frame produces a sequence that flickers, loses effect details, and yields a different mesh at every video frame. Our method addresses these failures by conditioning a video model on a render of the mesh and the prompt to obtain a reference video, then running a frozen image-to-3D generator on the video with two changes. The conditioning of each frame is blended over a temporal window, and low-rank adapters are fit per shape to restore the lost details. The mesh is encoded once for the whole sequence, so geometry is constant by construction, and the output is a single mesh with a texture per frame. Applied to various objects and effects, DynaMesh substantially improves over recent video-to-4D and texturing methods, and can generalize its temporal effect to different shapes never seen during training. Our project page is at https://threedle.github.io/dynamesh/.

</details>


### [134] [Deep Prior Learning for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05531)
*Yimou Wu,Jiaxin Guo,Yun-hui Liu,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VPGGT框架，通过先验残差连接与度量全局注意力，在含噪声位姿先验下提升具身感知的几何预测精度与物理尺度恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统需要超越纯图像的几何感知，现有前馈3D模型虽引入位姿、内参、深度等几何先验，但在处理噪声位姿、保全准确先验以及恢复物理尺度方面仍存在不足，简单接受这些输入并不足够。

Method: 基于VGGT扩展OmniVGGT，构建先验感知框架。针对相机位姿设计源自传感器特性的退化形式（内参与深度不做额外损坏）；引入无参数的先验残差连接（PRC）缓解"先验稀释"；并提出度量全局注意力，将全局尺度token条件化于可用的位姿与深度尺度，预测共享的度量缩放因子。

Result: 在四个数据集上，当为所有视图提供相机先验时，无论位姿精确还是损坏，PRC相比匹配的训练基线均提升了平移方向精度和联合位姿AUC。

Conclusion: 在细化阶段显式接入先验，是特征级条件化之外的有效补充，支持几何先验在具身感知中的更好利用。

Abstract: Embodied systems need geometric perception that exploits available observations beyond images alone. Recent feed-forward 3D models incorporate geometric priors, including camera poses, intrinsics, and depth. However, handling noisy poses, preserving accurate priors, and recovering physical scale require more than simply accepting these inputs. We introduce \emph{Vision-Prior Geometry Grounded Transformer} (VPGGT), a VGGT-based framework that extends OmniVGGT for prior-aware embodied perception. We formulate sensor-motivated pose corruptions from ground-truth trajectories for training and introduce a parameter-free \emph{prior residual connection} (PRC) to mitigate \emph{prior dilution}, where predictions are less accurate than their supplied pose priors. Our noise formulation targets camera poses; supplied intrinsics and depth receive no additional corruption. We further introduce \emph{Metric Global Attention}, which conditions a global scale token on available pose and depth scales and predicts a shared metric scaling factor for the geometric outputs. Experiments across four datasets show that \emph{PRC} improves translation-direction accuracy and joint pose AUC over a matched training baseline when camera priors are provided for all views, under both exact and corrupted poses. These results support explicit prior access during refinement as a useful addition to feature-level conditioning.

</details>


### [135] [Monocular markerless biomechanics for clinically interpretable gait assessment in spinal cord injury](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05552)
*Shreyasvi Natraj,Mathieu Ruepp,Yanke Li,Robert Riener,Inge Eriks-Hoogland,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建了脊髓损伤（SCI）步态数据集SCAI SCI Gait（239名成人），通过单目视频驱动的无标记流程重建下肢运动学与地面反作用力，验证其与动作捕捉/测力台高度一致，并揭示波形成分与功能独立性关联及速度分层下的机械策略差异，确立单目视频作为SCI可扩展临床生物力学评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维步态分析对脊髓损伤康复具有指导价值，但依赖标记点动作捕捉与测力台，大多数临床机构并不具备。单目无标记流程此前仅在少数健康成人队列中建立，尚未在神经系统疾病队列中验证。因此需要构建面向SCI人群的数据集与流程，实现可扩展的临床步态评估。

Method: 发布SCAI SCI Gait数据集，包含239名SCI成人，同步采集视频、动作捕捉与测力台数据。将参数化人体网格拟合到单个矢状面视角视频，通过虚拟标记驱动按人体测量学缩放的OpenSim模型，从而获得无标记下肢运动学，并据此预测地面反作用力；进一步采用条件依赖图分析与马尔可夫毯进行波形成分关联分析，并进行速度分层聚类。

Result: 无标记下肢运动学与动作捕捉达到当前最优一致性（r = 0.68–0.90，p < 0.001，RMSE = 4.18–6.49 度）；基于运动学预测的地面反作用力与测力台测量高度吻合（r = 0.85–0.87，p < 0.001，RMSE = 2.13–2.19 N/kg）。条件依赖图分析显示波形成分与功能独立性存在条件关联；速度分层聚类揭示在相近步速下个体间存在不同的机械策略。

Conclusion: 该工作证明单目视频可作为可扩展且具临床意义的生物力学评估与数据驱动表型分型手段，适用于脊髓损伤患者，为缺乏动作捕捉设备的临床场景提供了可行替代方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional gait analysis guides rehabilitation after spinal cord injury but depends on marker-based motion capture and force plates, which few clinics have. Monocular markerless pipelines have been established in fewer healthy adult cohorts but not in neurological cohorts. We present the SCAI SCI Gait dataset, comprising 239 adult individuals with spinal cord injury with synchronized video, motion capture, and force-plate measurements, we fitted a parametric body mesh to a single sagittal-view video, driving an anthropometrically scaled OpenSim model via virtual markers. Markerless lower-body kinematics showed state-of-the-art agreement with motion-capture measurements (r = 0.68-0.90, p < 0.001, and RMSE = 4.18-6.49 degrees), and accurate kinematics-based predicted ground-reaction forces closely matched those measured by force plates (r = 0.85-0.87, p < 0.001, and RMSE = 2.13-2.19 Newton per kg). Furthermore, conditional-dependence graph analysis with Markov blankets revealed that waveform components were conditionally associated with functional independence, and speed-stratified clustering revealed distinct mechanical strategies among individuals walking at similar speeds. These findings establish the use of monocular video as a scalable approach for clinically meaningful biomechanical assessment and data-driven phenotyping in patients with spinal cord injury. Github: https://github.com/SCAI-Lab/SCAI-SCI-Gait

</details>


### [136] [SteadySplats: Resampling of Low-Variance Gaussians for High-Fidelity Stochastic Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05576)
*Felix Windisch,Thomas Köhler,Lukas Radl,Chris Wyman,Georgios Kopanas,Bernhard Kerb,Markus Steinberger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套在表征与图像合成层面抑制噪声的原则性方法，使随机顺序无关透明渲染在低采样率下也能高效、无可见噪声地渲染3D高斯泼溅等辐射场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机顺序无关透明渲染能高效、优雅地渲染3D高斯泼溅等基于图元的辐射场，但输出固有的可见噪声使其难以实用，亟需在表征与图像合成层面系统性地降低高频噪声。

Method: 渲染阶段采用基于历史的空域重采样大幅加速图像收敛，并引入时域重要性重采样保证相机移动下的时间一致性；训练阶段使用颜色正则项隐式降低3DGS模型沿视线的方差；并实现基于Vulkan的优化渲染器。

Result: 在每像素1采样率下，相比已有随机方法PSNR提升约13 dB；可快速收敛至排序3DGS的效果，平均L1误差小于10⁻⁴；在高、低采样数下均能有效抑制输出噪声。

Conclusion: 通过在表征和图像合成层面协同抑制噪声来源，随机顺序无关透明渲染可在低采样预算下达到接近精确排序渲染的质量，使该方法更具实用性。

Abstract: Stochastic order-independent transparency enables efficient and elegant rendering of primitive-based radiance fields like 3D Gaussian Splatting models, but remains impractical due to the inherent visible noise in the output. We propose a principled approach to minimize high-frequency noise, addressing its sources at the representation and image synthesis level. During stochastic rendering, our history-based spatial resampling scheme drastically accelerates image convergence, while temporal importance resampling ensures coherence under camera movement. During training, a color regularizer implicitly reduces the variance along view rays in the 3DGS models. With these properties, our optimized, Vulkan-based renderer effectively mitigates output noise at low and high sample counts, achieving a substantial 13~dB PSNR increase in quality over previous stochastic methods at 1 sample per pixel and quickly converging to sorted 3DGS with an average L1 error of less than $10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [137] [Generating the Wild: Individual-Consistent Image-to-Video Generation for Wildlife](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05587)
*Yuzhuo Li,Di Zhao,Xinyu Zhang,Daniel Wilson,Yun Sing Koh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WildIcon，一个高频引导的图像到视频生成框架，用于提升野生动物个体一致性，并构建WildlifeVid数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物个体识别面临数据稀缺问题，同一动物在不同姿态、视角和运动下的观测难以获取。现有I2V模型侧重全局布局、语义和运动，难以保留区分个体的细粒度局部外观线索（如毛发纹理、条纹边界、斑点配置、轮廓过渡），而这些身份关键线索与高频信息密切相关。

Method: WildIcon引入频率感知的身份编码分支，从参考图像中提取个体特异的高频线索；结合隔离的前景信息，将生成的identity tokens作为身份条件注入cross-attention模块。基于冻结主干网络和轻量级身份适配，在保留参考图像细粒度身份线索的同时维持基础I2V模型的运动可控性与语义保真度。此外构建WildlifeVid数据集，包含高质量、时间连贯的视频片段和个体级身份标签。

Result: 在I2V生成和下游动物重识别（ReID）任务上的实验表明，WildIcon比现有基线具有更强的个体一致性，且其过滤后的输出可作为下游ReID的有用候选训练增强数据。

Conclusion: WildIcon通过高频引导有效提升了野生动物I2V生成的个体一致性，并验证了生成数据对下游ReID任务的增强价值。

Abstract: Individual-level wildlife identification often suffers from data scarcity, as varying observations of the same animal under diverse poses, viewpoints, and motions are rarely available. Image-to-video (I2V) generation offers a promising way to mitigate this limitation by synthesizing additional observations from a single reference image. However, existing I2V models mainly emphasize global layout, semantics, and motion, and therefore often fail to preserve fine-grained local appearance cues that distinguish one wildlife individual from another, such as fur texture, stripe boundaries, spot configurations, and contour transitions. We observe that these identity-critical cues are closely related to high-frequency information. To address this challenge, we propose WildIcon, a high-frequency-guided I2V framework for wildlife individual consistency. Specifically, WildIcon introduces a frequency-aware identity encoding branch that extracts individual-specific high-frequency cues from the reference image. Combined with isolated foreground information, the resulting identity tokens are then injected into cross-attention blocks as identity conditioning. Building on a frozen backbone with lightweight identity adaptation, WildIcon preserves fine-grained identity cues visible in the reference image while retaining the motion controllability and semantic fidelity of the base I2V model. In addition, to support the training and evaluation of wildlife individual-consistent I2V, we construct WildlifeVid, a wildlife-centric video dataset with high-quality, temporally coherent clips and individual-level identity labels. Experiments on I2V generation and downstream animal re-identification (ReID) show that WildIcon achieves stronger individual consistency than existing baselines, and that its filtered outputs can serve as useful candidate training augmentations for downstream ReID.

</details>


### [138] [EchoDino: A pediatric foundation model for transferable echocardiographic analysis across the lifespan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05603)
*Sheng Cheng,Donnchadh M. O'Sullivan,Daniel J. Penny,Craig G. Rusin,Minh B. Nguyen,Devika Subramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoDino是一个面向超声心动图的自监督基础模型，基于170万未标注儿童超声视频的370万帧训练，冻结编码器即可在多项任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图解读需整合全局解剖、局部结构与动态心脏运动信息；现有机器学习模型多为单一用途且依赖昂贵标注数据，在数据稀缺、解剖随年龄变化的儿科场景中尤为受限。

Method: 将DINOv3框架适配到儿童超声心动图数据，用170万未标注视频的370万帧进行自监督预训练；并提出运动偏置熵最大化采样（MEMS），为视频级分析挑选信息量最大的帧。

Result: 在九个儿童和成人数据集上，视图分类准确率从0.609提升至0.889；结构性心脏病检测AUC从0.811提升至0.872；年龄估计误差从3.857年降至1.389年；分割精度最佳，射血分数误差也更低。

Conclusion: EchoDino能从无标注儿童数据泛化到成人超声心动图，为全生命周期的心脏图像分析提供通用基础模型。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used cardiac imaging modality, yet interpretation demands integrating visual evidence across global anatomy, localized structures and dynamic cardiac motion. Machine-learning models have automated individual tasks, but they are typically built for a single purpose and depend on expensively labeled datasets - a barrier particularly acute in pediatric care, where data are scarce and anatomy changes with age. Here we present EchoDino, a self-supervised foundation model for echocardiography, created by adapting the DINOv3 framework to 3.7 million frames from 1.7 million unlabeled pediatric echocardiography videos. With its encoder frozen, EchoDino produces representations that capture global context, local anatomy, and dense spatial detail. We introduce Motion-biased Entropy Maximization Sampling (MEMS) to select the most informative frames for video-level analysis.
